[Recap] When the fake Holy Grail broke apart, you talked with Aventurine, Boothill, and the three Servants. Lancer and Archer said their goodbyes to their Masters before slowly disappearing from sight.

**Saber:** It's finally over...

> **Saber：**终于，这一切都结束了……

**Boothill:** Is this Grand Theater ever gonna see a day of peace...? I'm starting to feel bad for the Bloodhounds and the Dreamweavers.

> **波提欧：**多灾多难的大剧院，我都有些心疼匹诺康尼的猎犬和筑梦师了。

**Aventurine:** No need to feel bad for a heritage building insured with hundreds of different policies. Old Oti would never allow himself to lose a single coin over that.

> **砂金：**不必为了一栋被保上了几百种保险的古老建筑物操心…老奥帝这家伙从来不会在钱的问题上让自己吃亏。

**Lancer:** So he dragged everyone into this Holy Grail War while hiding in the shadows, and none of you are gonna make him pay for it?

> **Lancer：**哼，把所有人都卷进了这场「圣杯战争」里，自己却躲在幕后，难道没人打算去找他讨个说法？

- ▸ We should give him a good thrashing!
- ▸ Make him pay for our emotional damage!

**Aventurine:** Unfortunately, no Master was harmed in the making of this show. Even if we tried to hold him accountable, he'd probably just brush it off with his usual tricks — the best legal team money can buy and sky-high compensation.

> **砂金：**很遗憾，没有任何一位御主在这次事件中受伤，就算是向他问责，怕是也只会被他用他最熟悉的两样手段——最顶尖的律师团队，还有天价的赔偿给糊弄过去。

**Aventurine:** And... I fear the Holy Grail War has already given him returns far beyond what he's put into it.

> **砂金：**而「圣杯战争」的举行，显然已经让他得到了远超这些成本的收益。

**Aventurine:** The silver lining is that this little spectacle will serve as the perfect excuse during negotiations for Madam Jade to pry open an even bigger opportunity in Penacony.

> **砂金：**不过在谈判场上，这场闹剧会是个好用的借口，为翡翠女士在匹诺康尼撬开更大的入口。

- ▸ Does Skott have no rights?

**Aventurine:** Mr. Skott's issues will be handled by the IPC.

> **砂金：**斯科特先生的麻烦当然该由公司同僚来操心才是。

- ▸ Do Servants have no rights?

**Archer:** Unfortunately, we are indeed not protected by human law.

> **Archer：**很遗憾，我们确实不受人类法律的保护。

- ▸ Tch, so the IPC is still the final winner of it all.

**Aventurine:** Don't say it like that. This is a good thing for Penacony's shareholders.

> **砂金：**可别这么说，这对匹诺康尼的股东来说可是一桩好事啊。

**Boothill:** Hmph. These Penaconian rules stinkin' of money won't keep me down. Lancer, let's go take care of that old fudger.

> **波提欧：**哼，匹诺康尼这块铜臭之地的规矩管不了我。Lancer，我们去收拾收拾那个老可爱。

**Lancer:** Master, while I'm all in on that suggestion, time's running out. Without the Holy Grail holding us up, us Servants need to go back to where we belong.

> **Lancer：**御主，虽然我双手双脚赞成你的观点，但时间似乎已经不够了。圣杯解体后，我们也该回到原来的地方了。

- ▸ Are you leaving already?
- ▸ Who are you again?

**Boothill:** Such a shame, brother... If we ever cross paths again, I'll take you to the finest honky-tonk in the cosmos for a drink.

> **波提欧：**有些遗憾啊，哥们儿…如果还有机会见面，我一定请你去银河里最好的酒吧来两杯。

**Lancer:** Finest in the cosmos, huh? That's something to look forward to. Alright, no need for a tearful farewell for us bros, let's not ruin the good run we've had together. Till we meet again, buddy.

> **Lancer：**银河最好的酒吧吗？那可真令人期待。行啦，咱俩就不说什么告别的话了，别让这场难得的相聚染上悲伤的颜色。下次见，兄弟。

**Lancer:** And you two... Seriously, why can I never get a proper fight with you?

> **Lancer：**还有你们两个，哎，真是的，每次都不能和你们痛痛快快地打上一场。

**Saber:** Is that so? I distinctly remember beating you.

> **Saber：**是吗？明明我记得某人曾是我的手下败将来着？

**Archer:** You know me, just a humble archer. Let's call this one another loss on me.

> **Archer：**你知道的，我就是个普通的弓兵，就当这回我又输给你了吧。

**Lancer:** Tch, I'm done arguing with you lot. I'm gonna take another peek at Penacony's views before leaving for good.

> **Lancer：**切，懒得和你们争了——在消失之前，我想再去看看匹诺康尼的风景。

**Archer:** And so, it's time to say goodbye. Master, I have to admit that your fashion sense could use a little work, but... as a Servant, I'm genuinely glad to have fought by your side in this Holy Grail War.

> **Archer：**那么也该是告别的时候了。御主，不得不说，虽然你的着装品味有待提升，但…作为从者，很高兴这一次圣杯战争能有你同行。

**Archer:** I can still see the determined heart of the child you once were underneath all your grand speeches and extravagant flair. You know, it would feel pretty good to be a hero of justice once in a while.

> **Archer：**夸夸其谈和华丽的装饰下，我能看见一个内心执着的孩子…偶尔做一回正义的伙伴，感觉还不错。

**Aventurine:** Ahem, let's skip the last-minute psychoanalysis, alright? And when it comes to fashion... You're not much better than me, "Guardian of Humanity."

> **砂金：**咳咳，告别的时候，精神分析什么的就免了吧。还有，论及衣品，咱们两个彼此彼此啊，「人类的守护者」！

**Archer:** That brings up a good point. Why would I, a "Guardian of Humanity," get summoned to a world without the Counter Force?

> **Archer：**说来我也很好奇，作为「人类守护者」的我为什么会被召唤到这样一个没有抑止力的世界来——

**Saber:** Archer, you should know why you were summoned here.

> **Saber：** Archer，你应该清楚自己被召唤来这里的原因。

- ▸ You were summoned as a Heroic Spirit.

**Archer:** A hero, huh? ...I'll chalk it up as a beautiful dream.

> **Archer：**英雄啊…就当是无端做了一场美梦吧。

A faint smile tugged at the man's cold face, before he vanished from sight. Soon, only the final pair of Master and Servant remained.

*(Unlock Achievement Modern Times)*

### Say goodbye to Saber

> *Even the stillest fate must one day move. At the end of every hero's journey lies the same return and curtain fall.*

*(Talk to Saber)*

[Recap] Saber was delighted she had responded to your summon and experienced Penacony. She believed you would eventually become a star that illuminates the entire cosmos. As the Luminflux dimmed, the young knight disappeared.

**Saber:** ...I'm glad I was summoned to this strange world and got to experience it with you.

> **Saber：**…很高兴能应你的召唤，来到这个奇妙的世界游历一番。

- ▸ I'm not great at farewells...
- ▸ Anything else you want to say to me?

**Saber:** I saw your fears in your dream... but Master, don't be burdened by the emotions that come with being human: the fear of the unknown, the dread of loss, or the regret of what you couldn't achieve.

> **Saber：**在你的梦境里，我看到了你的畏惧…但，御主，请不要在意自己身为人类应有的那些情感：对未知的惶惑，对失去的恐惧，还有对自己做不到某事的悔恨。

**Saber:** Even Heracles was just a child before becoming a hero.

> **Saber：**在成为英雄前，赫拉克勒斯也曾是个孩子。

**Saber:** You may not be strong enough now, but I have faith that you'll eventually become a star that illuminates the entire cosmos.

> **Saber：**即使现在的你还不够强大，我相信总有一天，你会成为照亮银河的星辰。

- ▸ Like you did?

**Saber:** If my existence can occasionally light the way forward for you... that would be more than I could hope for.

> **Saber：**如果我的存在能偶尔为你照亮前路…那真是再好不过了。

- ▸ Who's Heracles...

**Saber:** ...

> **Saber：**……

**Saber:** Master, please send my regards to the person you will become in the future.

> **Saber：**御主，替我向未来的你问好。

As the Luminflux dimmed, the valiant girl knight faded, vanishing just as she had arrived.

At the same time, in the Dreamflux Reef, the host of the Holy Grail War is sitting elsewhere watching the scenery with interest...

[Recap] After the Holy Grail, Old Oti struck a deal with Mr. Reca for the memory legacy from the War. He signed a document giving up the trademark registered under Asna's name and all ownership rights to her creative works.

**Old Oti:** The Holy Grail... broke. What a shame.

> **老奥帝：**圣杯…碎了啊，可惜。

**Old Oti:** People have pursued it, fought for it, made sacrifices for it, and died for it, much like they did for our own Penacony.

> **老奥帝：**人们追逐它，为它战斗，为它奉献，为它生死，就像是…我们的匹诺康尼。

**Old Oti:** They poured their dreams into Penacony to shape the planet into what it is today. But the question is... has Penacony ever answered their dreams?

> **老奥帝：**他们将自己的梦注入其中，让匹诺康尼成为了如今的模样。那么，匹诺康尼有没有回应他们的愿望呢？

**Old Oti:** Look at this Memoria Singularity. Ten Amber Eras have passed, and out of those who once came here to build Penacony from the ground up, only one despicable old man is left. His friends, his enemies... they're all gone.

> **老奥帝：**看看这个忆质黑洞吧，十个琥珀纪过去了，曾经坐在这里，从零开始建造匹诺康尼的人们，如今就只剩下一个卑鄙无耻的老头。他的朋友也好，他的敌人也罢，全都不在了。

**Old Oti:** I must confess to both of you, my wish has been granted. No matter how you see me, no matter how deeply we hated each other in the past, the Holy Grail has recognized your achievements and brought you forth as Heroic Spirits...

> **老奥帝：**我必须向两位坦白，我的愿望实现了：无论你们如何看我，无论我们曾如何憎恨对方，圣杯承认了各位的功绩，也让你们以英灵的面貌现身……

**Old Oti:** Don't you think this is a rather unique reunion of old friends? It's unfortunate that Mikhail, that old rascal, couldn't be here to join us.

> **老奥帝：**两位难道不觉得，这也是一种别开生面的故友重逢吗？可惜，米哈伊尔这老小子却没能在场。

**Grady:** Mr. Oti, your wish truly moves me.

> **葛瑞迪：**奥帝先生，您的愿望真是让我感动。

**Grady:** If I didn't know you better, I'd almost be swayed by your sweet words. A reunion of old friends? No. It's just another part of your business deal.

> **葛瑞迪：**要不是我认识你，就要被你这鬼话唬住了。故友重逢？不过是你生意的一些添头罢了。

**Old Oti:** Grady, or perhaps I should now address you as Mr. Reca, is the editing of the footage complete? ...That is such an important task, after all.

> **老奥帝：**葛瑞迪…呃，不，也许此刻我该称呼你为芮克先生才对？作品剪辑完成了吗？…这么重要的工作，还得看你啊。

**Mr. Reca (?):** I've cut out everything related to the participants' privacy and kept only the most thrilling parts of the Holy Grail War. It may not be an artistic masterpiece, but it should meet your commercial expectations.

> **芮克先生（？）：**我剪去了涉及参赛者隐私的内容，保留了这场「圣杯战争」最精彩的部分。也许它在艺术性上谈不上是什么优秀的作品…但在商业性上，它应当不会令您失望的。

**Old Oti:** That'd be enough for me. You artists always aim to carve your names into art's annals no matter the cost... Too bad those who actually succeed are as rare as the winners of the Holy Grail War.

> **老奥帝：**那就够了，你们这些艺术家，总想着在自己信奉的艺术丰碑上不惜一切代价凿下自己的名字…嚯嗬嗬嗬，可惜啊，如愿以偿者就像圣杯战争的胜利者一样少之又少吧？

**Old Oti:** I'm not like that. What else can truly measure the effort you've put in apart from cold hard cash?

> **老奥帝：**我就不一样了，除开金钱回报，世上还有什么能更好地度量你为一件事所做的努力呢？

**Mr. Reca (?):** ...The memories of former Heroic Spirits are also worthy compensation.

> **芮克先生（？）：**…属于往昔「英灵」的记忆，也是一桩令我满意的报酬。

**Old Oti:** Memories of a flop director like Grady? Do enlighten me, what worth do those hold?

> **老奥帝：**喔？葛瑞迪那样的烂片导演的记忆？你倒是说说，那东西有什么价值？

**Mr. Reca (?):** His memories are indeed positively worthless as a filmmaker, but when it comes to him as a person... those memories are treasures beyond compare.

> **芮克先生（？）：**若论电影人的记忆…其价值确实贱如尘埃。但若论人的记忆，那真是…一件无与伦比的珍物。

**Mr. Reca (?):** He has had his struggles, disappointments, and defiance... only to be reduced to another nameless nobody. That man is a symbol of the Penacony of his times, a most ridiculous clown... And a most genuine hero.

> **芮克先生（？）：**挣扎、失意、抗争…最终又沦为无名的过客，这位前辈是那个匹诺康尼时代的缩影，是令人捧腹绝倒的小丑，也是货真价实的英雄。

**Mr. Reca (?):** How could I ever miss out on a memory with as many facets as a veritable diamond?

> **芮克先生（？）：**这样如宝石般有着众多切面的记忆，我岂能错失？

**Mr. Reca (?):** But now I'm more curious about you, sir. You've spent so much money, only to create such an unsightly mess. Aren't you worried you'll end up in disgrace like Grady?

> **芮克先生（？）：**不过，我更好奇的倒是您，所费不赀，却铺陈出这么大一出闹剧，就不怕像葛瑞迪那般落得个不体面的下场？

**Old Oti:** A merchant at my age who still cares about dignity would be an absolute failure.

> **老奥帝：**一个商人，如果活到我这个岁数还在乎体面，那他也太失败了。

**Mr. Reca (?):** In that case, my business here is settled. I'll take my leave.

> **芮克先生（？）：**那么，这笔生意两清了，容我告退。

**Old Oti:** Alright. It's your turn, Miss Asna. I'll sign the declaration to renounce the trademark on your name and all rights to your creative works... Are you happy with that?

> **老奥帝：**好了，该你了，阿斯娜小姐。我会签署声明，放弃你的名字注册而成的商标，还有对你一切创作成果的所有权的…这样，你满意了吗？

**Asna:** Happy? Never. But if you don't carry it through, I'll squeeze your puny neck with every ounce of my strength until I disappear... I'll watch you take your last breath, even if I know it won't actually kill you!

> **阿斯娜：**谈不上什么满意。但如果你敢说个不字，我会在消失前用尽全力掐住你那短到看不见的脖子，看着你咽气…虽然我明白，这样做根本杀不死你。

**Asna:** Goodbye, Penacony's most successful merchant. There's something I've always wanted to say to you —

> **阿斯娜：**再见了，匹诺康尼最成功的商人。我有句真心话一直想对你说——

**Asna:** I wish you were already dead.

> **阿斯娜：**早点去死吧！

**Old Oti:** Hahaha! Now that's a heartfelt confession! Asna, I do hope your wish comes true one day.

> **老奥帝：**嚯嗬嗬嗬！真是感人的告白哟！阿斯娜，希望你的愿望能有实现的那天。

At the same time, aboard the Express.

[Recap] On the Express, Pom-Pom chatted with Mikhail, the Heroic Spirit they summoned. Since the Holy Grail was destroyed, Misha had to go back to Penacony. Pom-Pom promised the Express would sound its whistle to honor the Nameless passengers who had died.

Far from the heat of battle, a Servant summoned under the Rider class stays on the sidelines. From the very beginning, it seems he had no intention of fighting.

As a Rider, he once built an unsinkable little ship named The Compass. As a Rider, he was once a Nameless who rode the stars aboard an interstellar Express.

Centuries have passed. Now, as a Heroic Spirit, the young man finally returns to the place where he once worked tirelessly. An old friend awaits him here.

**Pom-Pom:** ...March 7th is always bringing back bizarre and curious things. One time, she even brought back something called a "Rating Pistol," and it only gave Pom-Pom a single point!

> **帕姆：**…三月七乘客总爱带些稀奇古怪的东西回来，有次甚至还拿了把叫做「定分枪」的奇物，结果只给帕姆打了一分！

**Pom-Pom:** Also, watch out for Himeko's coffee. It's the most terrifying beverage Pom-Pom's ever encountered — but please don't ever tell her that to her face.

> **帕姆：**还有，要小心姬子乘客的咖啡，那是帕姆见过最可怕的饮料——但这事绝对不要当面告诉她。

**Misha:** Is it worse than the tea from Granholm's hometown?

> **米沙：**比格兰霍姆家乡的茶还难喝？

**Pom-Pom:** A hundred times worse!

> **帕姆：**难喝一百倍帕！

**Misha:** Pfft, hahaha. I'd love to have a try!

> **米沙：**噗，哈哈哈。真想品尝一口啊！

**Pom-Pom:** And! And! (Trailblazer) is always dragging March 7th into trouble. Pom-Pom had such high hopes for Dan Heng, but even he's started to follow along now.

> **帕姆：**还有还有，开拓者总是拉着三月七乘客一起胡闹，现在，就连帕姆最看好的丹恒乘客，都快被带坏了。

**Misha:** Mm-hmm.

> **米沙：**嗯嗯。

**Pom-Pom:** You passengers are all the same — Mikhail, you're spacing out again. What's going through that head of yours?

> **帕姆：**哎，你们这些乘客，简直都一个样——米哈伊尔乘客，你又在发呆了，你在想什么？

**Misha:** Conductor, looks like it's time to say our goodbyes again.

> **米沙：**列车长，看来，又到了该告别的时刻了。

**Pom-Pom:** Will you... be returning to the Express?

> **帕姆：**你…还会回列车来吗？

**Misha:** Maybe... I'm just happy to hear that so many people like me have boarded the Express over the years.

> **米沙：**大概吧。听帕姆说起这些年来，许多像我一样的人登上列车，真是…太好了。

**Misha:** The Nameless come and go. Some find a place they want to stay while others keep moving forward, just like the silver rails that never stop... That's the Astral Express. I'm sure the conductor's future will never be lonely.

> **米沙：**无名客们来来去去，有些人找到了他们的归宿，而有些人继续前行，就像银轨永远向前…这就是星穹列车。我相信，列车长的未来是不会寂寞的。

**Misha:** The Holy Grail War is coming to an end, and wandering souls must return to their rest. I... need to go back to Penacony.

> **米沙：**「圣杯战争」要结束了，漂泊的灵魂也该回到休憩中了。米沙…要回匹诺康尼了。

**Pom-Pom:** Mikhail, remember: whether you leave or stay, the Astral Express will always be the home of the Nameless. Pom-Pom will always be here... waiting for your return!

> **帕姆：**米哈伊尔乘客，要记得喔，无论你们是否离开，星穹列车是无名客永远的家。帕姆…会一直留在这儿，欢迎你们回来！

**Misha:** It's a deal then, Conductor. The next time the Express stops at Penacony... please sound the horn for me, Razalina, and Tiernan. If we can hear it... then we'll definitely come visit you.

> **米沙：**那就约好了，列车长。下一次停靠匹诺康尼时，请为我、为拉扎莉娜、为铁尔南…请为我们鸣笛。我们要是能听见，一定会回来见您。

**Pom-Pom:** It's a promise!

> **帕姆：**那就，一言为定帕！

[Recap] You and Robin chatted in front of the Holy Grail, discovering Sampo had sold it to Old Oti. Inside was a note with Sampo's wish: that everyone in the world would be happy, joyful, free, and have their every desire fulfilled.

Gather your thoughts to the present. If there really is a chance to fulfill a wish, what would yours be, (Trailblazer)?

- ▸ Make a silent wish.

**(Trailblazer):** You see, wishes lose their power once spoken aloud.

> **开拓者：**毕竟，愿望这种东西，如果说出来就不灵了。

**Robin:** That does sound like something you'd say.

> **知更鸟：**还真有你的性格。

**Robin:** Anyway, the first Holy Grail War orchestrated by Old Oti ended up being the last one.

> **知更鸟：**总之，老奥帝先生兴师动众的第一届「圣杯战争」也成了最后一届。

**Robin:** But the dream bubble movie co-produced by Mr. Reca and Dr. Edward became a huge hit. Not only did it fill up movie theaters in Epsilon, it even created a new dream-chasing trend throughout the stars. Mr. Oti and the IPC's Penacony branch made quite the fortune from it.

> **知更鸟：**不过，由芮克先生和爱德华医生协助制作的梦泡电影，不仅在艾普瑟隆大卖特卖，甚至再度在群星间掀起了逐梦热潮。奥帝先生和匹诺康尼的公司分部借着这趟热潮大赚了一笔。

- ▸ That old fox didn't lose a thing.
- ▸ He's far too shrewd.
- ▸ What about my appearance fee?

**Robin:** As Mr. Aventurine guessed, Oti simply brushed everything off as "entertainment" when questioned about placing Family heads and shareholders in danger.

> **知更鸟：**关于将家主和股东们牵扯进来一事，正如砂金先生料到的，绝大部分的事情，他都以「娱乐节目」的名义搪塞了过去。

**Robin:** While compensation and lawsuits are inevitable, I suspect he already knew exactly what was coming even before the Holy Grail was activated.

> **知更鸟：**天价的赔偿与官司自然是少不了的，但我怀疑，在圣杯启动前，他早就已经猜到会发生什么了。

**Robin:** Now, Old Oti did say he planned to give you some money, but even he thought that was too crude.

> **知更鸟：**老奥帝先生说，他原本打算单独付给你一笔赔偿金，但即使是他，也觉得这太庸俗了。

**Robin:** So instead, he gave us the most valuable thing from this Holy Grail War.

> **知更鸟：**所以，他把这场「圣杯战争」中最有价值的东西送给了我们。

- ▸ Too crude? No no no. Crude's good!
- ▸ So THIS is his apology?
- ▸ That old fox, always managing to get the most out of everything.

**Robin:** Hmm? There's something inside the Holy Grail — I think this is a note for you, (Trailblazer).

> **知更鸟：**嗯？圣杯里有什么东西——致开拓者，这张纸条好像是给你的。

**Message from Mr. Cold Feet:** Hey brother./sister. Since you all looked pretty busy, I've headed out with Hook. Big thanks to Mr. Oti for buying this rare trinket I got my hands on. That's how I could afford this spontaneous trip.

> **寒腿叔叔留言：**兄弟/姐们，看你们都挺忙的，我就带着虎克先走了。还得感谢老奥帝先生啊，他愿意买下我搞到手的这个稀罕货，咱们这才能有钱来一趟说走就走的旅行。

**Message from Mr. Cold Feet:** By the way, aren't you curious what old Sampo's wish to the Holy Grail was?

> **寒腿叔叔留言：**说起来，你难道不好奇，老桑博想对圣杯许下的愿望是什么吗？

- ▸ No.

**Message from Mr. Cold Feet:** I'm thinking you'll probably say "no." Owww, come on, bro!/sis! You might not be curious, but the audience is still on the edge of their seats!

> **寒腿叔叔留言：**我猜，你的回答肯定是「不好奇」。嗐，兄弟/姐们，你不好奇，观众们还期待着呢。

- ▸ I'm indeed curious.

**Message from Mr. Cold Feet:** I knew it, bro/sis! You're still thinking about old Sampo. And, I won't lie, the audience is waiting for it too.

> **寒腿叔叔留言：**我就知道，兄弟/姐们你还惦记着咱老桑博，不瞒你说，观众也都期待着呢。

**Message from Mr. Cold Feet:** Alright, folks! The show's over! But if there's anything still out there that can hear and fulfill my wish —

> **寒腿叔叔留言：**各位，节目已经完结了，但如果有什么能实现我的愿望——

**Message from Mr. Cold Feet:** "I wish for everyone in the world to be happy, joyful, free, and have their every desire fulfilled."

> **寒腿叔叔留言：**「我希望世上人人都幸福、快乐、自由，得偿所愿。」

*(Mission complete)*

*(Obtain Outlander King of Knights Puppet, Tarnished Glory)*

### Post-Mission

*(Listen to Old Oti and Skott)*

**Skott:** Mr. Oti...! I must've been insane to believe your nonsense!

**Skott:** I didn't just lose the Holy Grail. I got brutalized by a Galaxy Ranger! How are you going to compensate me for my lost hours, full attendance bonus, medical bills, and emotional distress?

**Old Oti:** Hohoho, take it easy, Mr. Skott. I always stick to one rule when it comes to business: I never let my partners take a loss.

**Skott:** You see, I traveled all this way and didn't get the time to bring anything special for you. This is a little plaster from the IPC hospital. Hope you'll like it.

**Old Oti:** Whether they're paying for Clockie, the Iris shows, or just shopping in the stores — every penny spent in Penacony ends with a little slice in my pocket.

**Old Oti:** More consumers — that's the fuel this so-called Holy Grail of Penacony truly runs on.

I knew it — wait, he agreed? Uh-oh. You didn't think he'd say yes. Now what? Are you really gonna touch Oti's fluff ball, friend?

## 050　Fate/Aria of Rotten Gold　命运/归还星之海洋

> 世界：Penacony　|　区域：Penacony Grand Theater　|　角色：Lancer; Old Oti; Asna; Robin; Boothill; Aventurine; Archer; Trailblazer; Saber; Himeko; Dan Heng; March 7th; Skaracabaz

### Head into the Grand Theater and unmask the mastermind

> *So this was their big move with the all-powerful Holy Grail? Now you're really curious about what kind of wish that stumpy old man is trying to make.*

[Recap] Lancer had followed the Holy Grail's energy trail to the Theater, confronting Old Oti and learning that the merchant had turned the entire Holy Grail War into a giant entertainment show. As Old Oti's ally, Miss Note suddenly appeared.

**Lancer:** It's a mockery of a war, but you set it up so intricately... I don't get it.

> **Lancer：**明明只是一场似是而非的闹剧，却有如此精妙的布置，这一点让我百思不得其解。

**Lancer:** The Twelve Hours that referenced the passage of time, and this "moon" placed right at the heart of it all.

> **Lancer：**隐喻时间变化的十二时刻，还有位居所有时刻中心的这枚「月亮」。

**Lancer:** Anyone with magecraft training can feel power continuously flowing into this place, but this world has no "magecraft" or "mages."

> **Lancer：**任何受过粗浅魔术训练的人都能感应到，力量正源源不断地聚流在此。但偏偏这个世界并没有所谓的「魔术」与「魔术师」。

**Lancer:** So, did you really design this whole Holy Grail War, old man? You don't exactly look like a mage.

> **Lancer：**所以，这场圣杯战争的一切真的都出自你的手笔吗，老爷子？你看起来并没有什么魔术师的气质啊？

**Old Oti:** Ohohoho! The sharp instincts of a businessman are enough for me to know where and when to make my moves, and the Holy Grail has told me everything I need to know.

> **老奥帝：**嚯嗬嗬嗬！凭一点商人本能的敏锐，就会知道该在哪儿布局落子。而我需要知道的，圣杯都告诉了我。

**Old Oti:** You are the Servant of that Galaxy Ranger... I didn't think your nose would be even sharper than the Bloodhound Family's minions.

> **老奥帝：**你…是那位巡海游侠的从者，没想到，你的嗅觉比猎犬家系的爪牙还要敏锐啊！

**Old Oti:** Is it really wise for you to charge in here on your own instead of loyally staying at your Master's side? That cowboy's in quite a pinch right now and would appreciate a speedy rescue...

> **老奥帝：**对了，你不回到御主身边为他忠心奋战，而是单骑突入，这样好吗？那个牛仔现在正身陷囹圄，急需你的拯救啊……

**Lancer:** What a pain. If I blow your head off right here, that'd save my metal clump of a Master too, no?

> **Lancer：**真是不省心啊。要是我在这儿打爆你的脑袋，也就等于解救了我那位铁疙瘩御主，对吧？

**Lancer:** Let me offer you some advice from experience — the Holy Grail never grants wishes the way people expect. All it leaves behind are dead souls buried by their desires.

> **Lancer：**作为过来人，让我给你个忠告吧——圣杯从不会如人所愿，它能留下的，只有被欲望掩埋的亡灵。

**Old Oti:** Ohohoho! I appreciate the advice, truly. In that case, am I not giving the Holy Grail a new purpose? It'll become an entertainment spectacle much more worthy of investment than some wish-granting chalice.

> **老奥帝：**嚯嗬嗬嗬！十分感谢阁下的告诫。所以，我这不正在为圣杯赋予新的价值吗？一档比许愿机更值得投资的娱乐节目。

**Lancer:** Heh, "entertainment spectacle"... I get it now. That clicks pretty well with the essence of the Holy Grail. No wonder it managed to power this Holy Grail War.

> **Lancer：**哼，「娱乐节目」…原来如此，这玩意儿与圣杯相性不错，也难怪能启动这场圣杯战争。

**Lancer:** I've been wondering what you could possibly put into the Holy Grail apart from wine, given this is a barren world where Mystics have completely degraded.

> **Lancer：**我一直在想，在这个神秘衰退的世界里，如此贫瘠的土地上，就算手握圣杯，除了往里倒酒外什么也做不成吧？

**Lancer:** But if something could replace magical energy, and manage to infuse the Holy Grail with enough power to create miracles... well, that changes things entirely.

> **Lancer：**如果有什么东西能取代魔力，为圣杯灌注创造奇迹的热量…那就另当别论了。

**Lancer:** Instead of something that must maintain the Concealment of Mystics, the Holy Grail War will become a public entertainment spectacle instead.

> **Lancer：**原本应当保持「神秘的隐匿」的「圣杯战争」，摇身一变成了公开的娱乐节目。

**Lancer:** Then, the people on this dream-soaked planet will come to believe in the miracle of the Holy Grail through learning its rituals and witnessing the summoning of Heroic Spirits.

> **Lancer：**而这个浸泡在梦中的星球上，人们因为知晓圣杯的仪式、见证英灵的召唤，相信了圣杯奇迹的存在。

**Lancer:** And so, little by little, their wishes will turn into fuel, coating the Holy Grail in a fabricated miracle — and the farce you've orchestrated will end up stomping all over those wishes!

> **Lancer：**于是，他们点点滴滴的愿望化作了燃料，为圣杯镀上一层虚造的奇迹——而你所主持的这场闹剧，践踏了他们的愿望！

**Old Oti:** Quite the impressive insight, Servant from another world. Your presence here was certainly a surprise to me, but you and your friends are needed. The Holy Grail needs more efficient fuel — and that means... you.

> **老奥帝：**真是了不得的见识，异界的从者，你的出现确实让我有些意外。但你们的到来是必要的，圣杯需要更高效的燃料，也就是你们——

**Old Oti:** Miss Note... No, ████. It's time for you to show Penacony your true charm.

> **老奥帝：**音符小姐，哦不，███，是时候向匹诺康尼展现你真正的魅力了。

The curtain fell with a soft rustle, veiling the stage in silence.

[Recap] Miss Note used her powers of the Harmony to trap Lancer. Old Oti tasked her with stalling you and the others, who were about to arrive, and entrusted her with the Holy Grail's accumulated power to do so.

**Miss Note:** I did what you wanted me to do...

> **音符小姐：**你要我做的事，我办到了……

**Old Oti:** Well done, my old friend. So many years have passed, but I still remember every performance you gave, every dance you danced, every melody you sang...

> **老奥帝：**辛苦了，老朋友。一眨眼这么多年过去了，我依然记得你在银幕上的每一场演出，跳过的每一段舞，唱出的每一支小调……

**Old Oti:** You were like a blooming iris, but I prefer how you are now compared to your heydays on the stage... stripped of all the external pretenses, so simple, so ordinary.

> **老奥帝：**那时候的你，就像一簇盛开的鸢尾花。不过，比起你正当盛年时的舞台风姿，我更喜欢如今这般模样…卸去了所有外在装饰，如此平凡的你。

**Miss Note:** Enough, Oti. Outdated metaphors are as revolting as last night's leftovers. I've kept my promise. Now it's your turn.

> **音符小姐：**够了，奥帝。过时的比喻就像隔夜餐点让我作呕。我已经履行了约定，到你了。

**Old Oti:** There's no rush, my old friend. Everything you desire can be obtained without magecraft or miracles — money is all it takes. The so-called Holy Grail is nothing more than a vessel crafted from gold.

> **老奥帝：**不急，老朋友。你想要的一切根本用不着魔术和奇迹，用钱就能办到——所谓圣杯，也不过是用黄金浇铸的容器。

**Old Oti:** And the real stars of this show are about to make their entrance. Your job is to hold them off until the last step of the ritual is complete. Once that is done, I will honor my end of the deal.

> **老奥帝：**而我们这出节目的主角们就要来了，你负责将他们拖住，直到仪式的最后一步完成。届时，我会履行约定的。

**Miss Note:** Oti, looks like your business acumen has rotted along with this world.

> **音符小姐：**奥帝，看来，你的商业头脑也跟这个世界一起腐烂了。

**Miss Note:** I could barely restrain that one Servant, and now you expect me to take them all on?

> **音符小姐：**我连困住刚才那名从者都力不从心，你居然想让我打败他们所有人？

**Old Oti:** Ohohoho! Don't be so modest. Unlike Grady, you're a true artist! You had the talent to bring thousands of characters to life all on your own. You are the perfect counter to these "heroes."

> **老奥帝：**嚯嗬嗬嗬！别妄自菲薄，和葛瑞迪不同，你可是真正的艺术家！在舞台上以一人演绎万千角色悲喜人生的才能，你正是这些「英雄」的克星。

**Old Oti:** I will give you the power the Holy Grail has accumulated so you can crush them all. Then, this grand spectacle will come to a glorious finale!

> **老奥帝：**我会将圣杯积累的力量托付给你，给予你压倒他们的筹码。如此一来，这场盛大的仪式必将迎来圆满的收场！

*(Approach the marked location)*

[Recap] You and the team arrived at the Grand Theater, only to find Miss Note standing in the center of the path. She unleashed her Noble Phantasm, plunging everyone into a deep sleep.

**Boothill:** Fork it, wasn't that blue guy supposed to scout this place ahead of us? Haven't even heard a single peep from him so far... Could that old man have pulled a fast one on him?

> **波提欧：**他宝贝的，那个蓝色的哥们儿不是说先来探路吗？他怎么连个回报都没有，该不会给那老可爱算计了吧？

**Saber:** No signs of battle. This doesn't quite seem like something Lancer would do.

> **Saber：**没有战斗过的痕迹，不太像Lancer的作风。

- ▸ Maybe he went fishing.
- ▸ Or is he lost?

**Archer:** From what I know about that man, he might even stir up some pointless fights, just to walk away happy...

> **Archer：**依照我对那个男人的了解，他甚至会挑起些没有意义的争执，然后心满意足地转身离开……

**Robin:** Miss Note! I'm glad you're okay—

> **知更鸟：**音符小姐！你没事就好——

**Aventurine:** Wait. Something's off. Miss Note, I trust that you've seen Lancer here, haven't you?

> **砂金：**等等，诸位，情况似乎不太对劲。音符小姐，你在这儿见过Lancer了吧？

**Miss Note:** Yes. He came to the Grand Theater with his spear raised and ready, eager to impale Old Oti on the curtain. I couldn't agree more with his approach...

> **音符小姐：**不错，他来到大剧院，打算直奔主题，用他手中的长枪把奥帝先生钉在大幕上。我对他的看法深表赞同……

**Miss Note:** But I still have dreams left unfulfilled in this miraculous second life... So, I asked him to take a break.

> **音符小姐：**不过，获得这第二次生命实在不易，我还有未实现的愿望。因此，我请他稍事休息片刻。

**Miss Note:** And I must insist that every one of you take a break as well... until Mr. Oti finishes the ritual.

> **音符小姐：**同时，我也不得不请各位小憩片刻……直到奥帝先生完成仪式为止。

**Miss Note:** Fear not. There will be no pain. Please leave your passion and ambitions on the stage, like how actors shed their fatigue and makeup behind the curtains... and rest.

> **音符小姐：**无需担心，不会有半点痛苦。请诸位剥离舞台上的炽烈激情与雄心壮志…就像帷幕后的演员们卸去脸上疲惫与妆容那样…好好休息。

*(Cutscene begins)*

[Recap] Miss Note unleashed her Noble Phantasm, her threads pulling you into the dark. "Let the curtain fall, Canon Cancrizans!"

**Miss Note:** Let the curtain fall, Canon Cancrizans!

> **音符小姐：**落幕吧，庸人的浮生咏叹调！

*(Cutscene ends)*

[Recap] Saber and you were sent into the illusion Caster wove. It was as if fate had rewound to the moment right before Saber pulled the sword out of the stone.

**Saber:** Where's this...?

> **Saber：**这里是……？

**Saber:** How... is this possible...? Why am I in Britain?

But, was that answer truly what you thought? Or was it some sort of illusive manipulation? You try to piece together what has happened, but your memories feel more distant with every passing second.

About the Astral Express and the Nameless. Like sand under the waves, your memories crumble and fade. And suddenly, you understand — why Saber chose to stay, even just for a moment.

Why didn't you step aboard the Astral Express back then, on that split-second impulse? It no longer matters. The researcher in the Department of Cosmic Geography standing before you watches you closely, perhaps searching for words of comfort, or maybe looking for the right joke to shatter the heavy silence. Eventually, he speaks.

**Lv. 4 Geography Dept. Researcher:** Well, your resume doesn't show any particular skills, so I'll start you off with some light tasks...

> **地概科Ⅳ级科员：**看你的简历似乎没什么特长，我先给你安排点轻松的工作好了……

**Lv. 4 Geography Dept. Researcher:** Sort the Department of Cosmic Geography's expenditure report from the past six months. Schedule research meetings for the upcoming month. Collect project proposals from all levels of researchers. Yeah, that should keep you busy for a while.

> **地概科Ⅳ级科员：**整理地概科过去半年的经费报表，预约未来一个月的研究会议，收集各级科员的课题申请…够你干上一段时间了。

- ▸ Am I really a researcher?

**Lv. 4 Geography Dept. Researcher:** What else would you be? Madam Herta?

> **地概科Ⅳ级科员：**要不然呢？你总不可能是黑塔吧？

- ▸ Isn't this the Department of Cosmic Geography?

**Lv. 4 Geography Dept. Researcher:** Don't be so surprised. Our department is at the bottom of the food chain on the station... this work happens all the time.

> **地概科Ⅳ级科员：**别大惊小怪，在空间站，我们地概科是食物链的底端，这种事情都是家常便饭啦。

**Lv. 4 Geography Dept. Researcher:** I'm off, (Trailblazer). Be sure to do your job well.

> **地概科Ⅳ级科员：**我走了，开拓者，派给你的活儿好好干啊。

He turns to leave... yet could not help himself from looking back at you.

**Lv. 4 Geography Dept. Researcher:** Yeah, sometimes I regret picking the Geography of the Universe course when I was fourteen and walking away from Quantum History... I felt math was just too tough for me back then. But now, I ask myself... Would I still be stuck in this dead-end job if I made a different choice?

> **地概科Ⅳ级科员：**我偶尔也会后悔自己十四岁那年没去进修「量子历史」而选择了「寰宇地概」，我当时觉得…数学太难了。我常常问自己，如果我做了别的选择，还会是今天这幅衰样吗？

**Lv. 4 Geography Dept. Researcher:** No no no. C'mon. Don't worry about the what-ifs. Why waste time regretting a choice you've already made? Look at me, things aren't so bad here, are they?

> **地概科Ⅳ级科员：**嗐，都是瞎想。新来的，别为已经做出的决定后悔。你看我，这样不挺好？

This is all wrong! You scream internally, but the fury is fleeting.

You're on the verge of shouting, but just then, a sliver of doubt creeps inside you.

Is this really what you want? Take a breath. Don't lash out. Focus on what you truly need to do.

- ▸ Take out the baseball bat and gave them a good beating.

The bat you always have at your side... is no longer there. Then again, did you ever really have it in the first place?

- ▸ I quit.

But where would you go? Is there anywhere you can go? You're young, with no special talents. Isn't your best bet a cushy job with full benefits, clocking in every day until the world ends?

- ▸ Sort by timeline.

Yes. That's it. Calmly and nicely, drop the fight. By now, the job feels like second nature. Might as well tidy up the last of the files.

### Talk to the strange man

**(Trailblazer):** C'mon, Uncle A, don't you ever feel like you don't belong here?

> **谜之科员A：**因为现在的生活不称心如意，就觉得自己应该属于其他地方，这不过是另一种美化了的逃避现实的借口罢了。

**Mysterious Researcher A:** Personally? I think this place isn't so bad. No world-threatening crises, no freaks trying to end humanity. The kitchen's convenient, too... though the ingredients are a bit odd.

> **谜之科员A：**依我看，这里不挺好的嘛。没有世界危机，没有想要毁灭人类的怪胎。厨房也很方便，除了食材有点古怪。

You glance at him, wanting to say something, but decided not to.

Days pass. After long hours of sorting, you're finally done with the work.

Although an uncanny feeling lingers in your chest, before it's resolved, you should take a break.

Is that really how you feel? If joining the Astral Express doesn't change your life for the better... What then?

What if what awaits you isn't a grand adventure, but endless cleaning, constant repairs, wounds that never heal, and death steeped in despair... What will you do then?

What if you can't help anyone? What if you become a dead weight on the Express? What if a Nameless gives their life to save you, yet everyone silently wishes you'd just leave... What will you do?

Think about it. What answer should you give?

**(Trailblazer):** I'll think about it... I'll make sure to give you my answer before the Express leaves.

**Himeko:** Alright. I'll respect your choice no matter what you pick.

> **姬子：**好啊，无论你想要留在哪里，我都会尊重你的选择。

[Recap] In your dream, you heard Miss Note wish upon the Holy Grail to reclaim her True Name. She refused to release her Noble Phantasm unless Robin used a Command Spell on her, but Robin bet that all of you could escape without it.

A peaceful dream, stirred by birdsong beyond the window. However, can a robin really sing in the vacuum of space?

**Miss Note:** Let me tell you the story of a bird who was imprisoned wrongfully. She was once a singer, thrown into a dark cell for composing songs that the powerful didn't like.

> **音符小姐：**让我们来说说一只鸟儿的故事吧，一只蒙冤入狱的鸟儿。她曾是一位歌手，因为谱写不受权贵们喜爱的歌谣而被投入铁牢。

**Miss Note:** The bird heard many voices of sorrow. So, she sang for the others trapped inside, soothing their homesick hearts and inspiring them to rise up.

> **音符小姐：**在囚笼中，鸟儿听到了许多悲叹的声音。于是，她为同在笼中的人们歌唱，抚慰他们的思乡愁绪，鼓舞他们起义的壮志。

**Miss Note:** In the end, the bird broke free, with a pocket watch, a wolf, and countless companions by her side.

> **音符小姐：**最终，鸟儿和一块怀表、一头狼还有无数同伴一起冲破了牢笼。

**Miss Note:** The bird believed that she would begin a new life after gaining freedom. She would shine on the stage and the silver screen, displaying her beautiful feathers and voice, just as she had once brought hope to the hopeless.

> **音符小姐：**鸟儿以为，赢得自由后她能开始全新的生活。她能在舞台上和银幕中展示自己美丽的羽毛与歌喉，像过去那样为一无所有的人们带去希望。

**Miss Note:** But... she was wrong. Penacony soon became unrecognizable. The songs she sang, the films she starred in, and even her own life were all locked away in a cage called money, turned into mere commodities.

> **音符小姐：**但…她错了。匹诺康尼很快变得面目全非。鸟儿所唱的歌曲，出演的电影，还有她自己的生活，全被关进了名为金钱的笼子里，成了商品。

**Miss Note:** The movie studio wanted to turn the bird into a symbol of beauty, but the bird longed to escape from this suffocating, filthy gutter...

> **音符小姐：**电影公司想将鸟儿打造成美丽的符号，鸟儿却想要飞离这片令它窒息的臭水沟……

**Miss Note:** They stripped away her stage name, replaced her with a fresh-faced chick, and smothered her with slander and rumors, leading her to a disgraceful end.

> **音符小姐：**于是他们夺走了它的艺名，找来一只全新的雏鸟替代银幕上的她，又用污浊的流言和臆想将她扼死在不名誉的结局里。

**Robin:** ...So, you seek revenge on those who tarnished your name?

> **知更鸟：**…所以，你想报复那些令你蒙受污名的人？

**Miss Note:** No. The exact opposite, in fact. When I returned to this world, I realized there was no one left to take revenge on.

> **音符小姐：**恰恰相反…再次回到这个世界时，我发现自己已经没有可以报复的对象了。

**Miss Note:** Instead, I will reclaim the name they took from me and erase every single work I've ever produced... I'll destroy every trace of my existence as ████.

> **音符小姐：**所以我要索回我被夺走的名字，抹消所有属于我的作品……让身为███时存在过的一切痕迹不复存在。

**Robin:** You know how it feels to be locked in a cage and have everything taken from you, so why do you still trap the Masters and Heroic Spirits in your Noble Phantasm? When they wish to end this Holy Grail War?

> **知更鸟：**既然曾经被囚禁，被夺取…那么如今您更不应该将所有为终结这场圣杯战争而来的御主与英灵困锁在宝具中。

**Robin:** Old Oti may be able to fulfill your wish, but the cost he demands is far greater. As someone born in the same era as him, you should know this better than I do.

> **知更鸟：**老奥帝先生或许能实现你的愿望，但他索要的却是更高昂的利益。这一点，和他曾同处于一个时代的你比我更清楚。

**Miss Note:** ...

> **音符小姐：**……

**Miss Note:** Oti is nothing more than a parasite feeding on Penacony... Of course I won't trust him. But you're different, Master. He's a worm who has a wish for the Holy Grail, yet there's nothing the Holy Grail can do to sway you.

> **音符小姐：**奥帝是只啃噬匹诺康尼的虫子，他当然不可信。但御主，他是只对圣杯有所求的虫子…而你，圣杯无法收买你的心智。

**Miss Note:** If you want to stop my Noble Phantasm, you'll have to force me with a Command Spell! Come, Master! Take away my free will, just like how they took away my name and career with their contracts and laws!

> **音符小姐：**想让我解除宝具？那就用令咒命令我吧！御主，像那些人用合同与法律夺走我的名字、事业一样，来吧！

**Robin:** You know I won't do that, Miss Note! ...Let's make a wager, though I've never once gambled in my life.

> **知更鸟：**你知道我不会这么做的，音符小姐！…我们来打个赌吧，虽然我这辈子从未和别人赌过什么。

**Robin:** I bet that those Masters and Servants will not yield to your Noble Phantasm, even though you've stripped their identity as heroes — I trust them.

> **知更鸟：**我打赌那些被你剥夺「英雄」妆容的御主与从者，绝不会被你的宝具困住——我对他们有信心！

**Miss Note:** ...I will wait and see.

> **音符小姐：**……那么，我将拭目以待。

The birdsong fades into silence.
